Note that the root Pages middleware also spans static asset requests

Description

The Cloudflare Pages setup at docs/platforms/javascript/guides/cloudflare/index.mdx:161-201 tells the reader to register sentryPagesPlugin as the first entry in onRequest inside functions/_middleware.js (snippet at :174). includes/cloudflare-pages-middleware-intro.mdx instructs creating that file in the functions directory at the project root. Neither says which requests the middleware then handles.

Verified: a root functions/_middleware.js runs on every Cloudflare Pages request, static asset requests included. Every asset fetch therefore produces its own span and transaction alongside the app's function routes, which changes both the trace volume and what the reader sees in the performance view.

What to change

  • Add a note to the Pages setup section stating that a root middleware runs on every request, static assets included.
  • Point readers at placing the middleware in a subdirectory when they want only their function routes instrumented.

Source: runtime docs audit, item DOC-R19

Status quo. docs/platforms/javascript/guides/cloudflare/index.mdx:161-201 tells the reader to add `sentryPagesPlugin` as the first middleware in `functions/_middleware.js`, and includes/cloudflare-pages-middleware-intro.mdx instructs creating that file in the `functions` directory at the project root. Neither says what requests the middleware then runs on.

On develop. Verified: a root `functions/_middleware.js` runs on every Cloudflare Pages request, static asset requests included, so those produce spans and transactions alongside the app's own routes.

Work item. Add a note to the Pages setup section stating that a root middleware runs on every request including static assets, and point readers at scoping the middleware to a subdirectory when they only want their function routes instrumented.

DOC-R20Bun OpenTelemetry setup includes import @sentry/node-core and call APIs that no longer existP0verified in source

Proposed issue docs-change

Rewrite the Bun OpenTelemetry setup includes against develop

Description

Three Bun OpenTelemetry includes are written against a package and APIs that no longer exist. platform-includes/performance/opentelemetry-setup/javascript.bun.mdx:5 and :48 import SentryContextManager, validateOpenTelemetrySetup from @sentry/node-core; error-monitoring-only/javascript.bun.mdx:3 and with-custom-sampler/javascript.bun.mdx:3 do the same. javascript.bun.mdx:12 and :55 pass skipOpenTelemetrySetup: true, and error-monitoring-only/javascript.bun.mdx:30 calls provider.addSpanProcessor(...).

On develop there is no node-core package under packages/. skipOpenTelemetrySetup, SentryContextManager, validateOpenTelemetrySetup and SentrySpanProcessor appear nowhere in packages/*/src. @sentry/opentelemetry exports only getScopesFromContext, SentryPropagator, SentryTracerProvider, setOpenTelemetryContextAsyncContextStrategy and registerPrepareSpanScope (packages/opentelemetry/src/index.ts:1-9). The Bun option is enableOpenTelemetrySetup (packages/bun/src/types.ts:23), and addSpanProcessor was removed in OpenTelemetry JS 2.x.

What to change

  • Drop every @sentry/node-core import and the removed symbols.
  • Replace skipOpenTelemetrySetup: true with enableOpenTelemetrySetup: false.
  • Replace provider.addSpanProcessor(...) with the constructor's spanProcessors array.

Source: runtime docs audit, item DOC-R20

Status quo. platform-includes/performance/opentelemetry-setup/javascript.bun.mdx:5 and :48 import `SentryContextManager, validateOpenTelemetrySetup` from `@sentry/node-core`; the siblings error-monitoring-only/javascript.bun.mdx:3 and with-custom-sampler/javascript.bun.mdx:3 do the same. javascript.bun.mdx:12 and :55 pass `skipOpenTelemetrySetup: true`, and error-monitoring-only/javascript.bun.mdx:30 calls `provider.addSpanProcessor(...)`.

On develop. There is no `node-core` package under packages/ on develop. `skipOpenTelemetrySetup`, `SentryContextManager`, `validateOpenTelemetrySetup` and `SentrySpanProcessor` appear nowhere in packages/*/src; `@sentry/opentelemetry` exports only `getScopesFromContext`, `SentryPropagator`, `SentryTracerProvider`, `setOpenTelemetryContextAsyncContextStrategy` and `registerPrepareSpanScope` (packages/opentelemetry/src/index.ts:1-9). The Bun option is `enableOpenTelemetrySetup` (packages/bun/src/types.ts:23), and `addSpanProcessor` was removed in OpenTelemetry JS 2.x.

Work item. Rewrite all three Bun includes against develop's surface: drop the `@sentry/node-core` imports and the removed `SentryContextManager` / `validateOpenTelemetrySetup` / `SentrySpanProcessor` symbols, replace `skipOpenTelemetrySetup: true` with `enableOpenTelemetrySetup: false`, and replace `provider.addSpanProcessor(...)` with the constructor's `spanProcessors` array.

Checked and dropped: candidate 3: The premise does not hold. `Sentry.cron` is exported from @sentry/bun (packages/bun/src/index.ts:109, re-exported from @sentry/node), and `instrumentCron`, `instrumentNodeCron` and `instrumentNodeSchedule` (packages/node/src/cron/{cron,node-cron,node-schedule}.ts) are explicit user-applied Proxy wrappers around a library object the user passes in — `instrumentCron(CronJob, 'slug')`, `instrumentNodeCron(cron)`, `instrumentNodeSchedule(schedule)`. They do not patch the packages via diagnostics channels or require-hooks, so they have no dependency on a Bun.build with `@sentry/bun/plugin`. The include at includes/javascript-crons-automatic-crons-instrumentation.mdx matches the shipped API, and nothing in the source contradicts the page.; candidate 9: The page is not misleading. docs/platforms/javascript/common/configuration/integrations/dedupe.mdx:2 and :7 already state "(default)" and "This integration is enabled by default", and platform-includes/configuration/dedupe/javascript.deno.mdx is the site-wide 'if you customized defaultIntegrations' snippet shape used by every default integration on every platform, not a Deno-specific claim. It is true that packages/deno/src/sdk.ts:37 installs `dedupeIntegration()` by default, but the docs say so, so there is no defect to fix here beyond a convention that spans the whole integrations section..

What this audit did not measure

Browser-side errors ran in one framework cell of 51. Release health, metrics, cron monitors, source maps, profiling, request bodies and streaming were probed per runtime, never inside a framework. No cell covers Cloudflare Pages Functions with a framework, Deno Deploy, a single-file executable per framework, a Durable Object or a scheduled handler inside a framework Worker, standalone Nitro, or @sentry/effect. Unprobed SDK features include the runtime-metrics integrations on Bun and Deno, the Bun cron helpers, the Cloudflare email and tail handlers, Workers AI spans, and fifteen default library integrations on Deno. The full list, with the docs pages never cited, is the section "What was not measured" in the deep probe report.

Checked, not a defect

N1Bun outgoing node:http and fetch requests are instrumented and propagatenot reproduced

gist B1 reported node:http clients uninstrumented on Bun. probes/PROP.mjs, PKG=@sentry/bun bun run PROP.mjs, bun 1.4.0, probes/results/PROP-bun.json: both /fetch and /http received sentry-trace and baggage, and both appear as http.client children of parent. Same as node. No docs change needed; DOC-B1 can say both clients are covered under bun run.

N2Importing @sentry/deno before the library does not block injectionnot reproduced

A first probe showed runtime: [] after await import('mysql') when @sentry/deno was imported first. The cause was the probe: mysql loads its instrumented lib/Connection.js on createConnection(), so the marker stays empty until the first connection. With that call (probes/D-HOOK2.mjs) the documented order (hook, SDK import, init(), library) injects on deno 2.9.0 and 2.8.3 (probes/results/D-HOOK.txt).

N3compatibility_date: 2024-09-23 with nodejs_compat boots the SDKnot reproduced

getting-started-config/javascript.cloudflare.mdx:5 states this floor. probes/CF/wrangler-olddate.jsonc returns the same nine integrations and propagated headers as a 2026 date. nodejs_als alone also boots (probes/results/CF-RUN.txt), so the include's claim is correct and DOC-C8 only asks the Next.js page to say its later date is OpenNext's.

N4The SvelteKit and Remix pages under frameworks/ do carry Cloudflare contentnot reproduced

A read pass reported frameworks/sveltekit.mdx and frameworks/remix.mdx as generic. Both pull getting-started-complete for their framework, and those includes gate Cloudflare sections: platform-includes/getting-started-complete/javascript.sveltekit.mdx:69-73,326-332 (initCloudflareSentryHandle) and javascript.remix.mdx:263-269. No item; the open tickets sentry-docs#14888 and #14890 ask for Workers wording, not for missing content.
